```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import Briefing from '@/features/explore/components/panel/Briefing'
import PanelHeader from '@/features/explore/components/panel/PanelHeader'
import SourceLine from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SourceLine'
import TypeBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/TypeBars'
import { GEUMAM, JUMUNJIN } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

describe('PanelHeader', () => {
  it('← 시군구 그래프 · 이름 · 바닷가 칩 · 시도 · 시군구', async () => {
    const onBack = vi.fn()
    render(<PanelHeader dong={JUMUNJIN} onBack={onBack} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('바닷가 0.0km')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('강원특별자치도 · 강릉시')).toBeInTheDocument()
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '← 강릉시 그래프' }))
    expect(onBack).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
  })
  it('내륙 동(coastKm null)은 바닷가 칩이 없다', () => {
    render(<PanelHeader dong={GEUMAM} onBack={() => {}} />)
    expect(screen.queryByText(/바닷가/)).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})

describe('TypeBars', () => {
  it('점수 높은 순 4행, 1위 강조, 막대 폭 = 점수 %, 이유 한 줄', () => {
    render(<TypeBars types={JUMUNJIN.types} />)
    const region = screen.getByRole('region', { name: '워케이션 유형' })
    const rows = within(region).getAllByRole('listitem')
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.querySelector('[data-name]')?.textContent)).toEqual([
      '장기체류형',
      '로컬살이형',
      '네트워킹형',
      '몰입형',
    ])
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveAttribute('data-top', 'true')
    expect(rows[1]).not.toHaveAttribute('data-top')
    expect(rows[0].querySelector('[data-fill]')).toHaveStyle({ width: '61%' })
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('숙박 성향 64 · 한산한 달 7개(성수기보다 20% 이상 적은 달)')
  })
})

describe('Briefing', () => {
  it('3문장을 한 문단으로, 칩·번호 없음', () => {
    render(<Briefing sentences={JUMUNJIN.briefing} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '이 동네에서 일하는 하루' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(JUMUNJIN.briefing.join(' '))).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByText(/F\d/)).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })
  it('빈 배열이면 준비 중 문구', () => {
    render(<Briefing sentences={[]} />)
    expect(screen.getByText('브리핑을 준비하고 있어요.')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})

describe('SourceLine', () => {
  it('문구를 그대로', () => {
    render(<SourceLine>데이터: LG U+ 유동인구 2025 · 갱신 2026-11-13</SourceLine>)
    expect(screen.getByText('데이터: LG U+ 유동인구 2025 · 갱신 2026-11-13')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/PanelHeader.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = { dong: DongDetail; onBack: () => void }

/** ② 헤더 (Figma 436:10454). 바닷가 칩은 coastKm ≤ 1 일 때만 (스펙 §3-3) */
export default function PanelHeader({ dong, onBack }: Props) {
  const coastal = dong.coastKm !== null && dong.coastKm <= 1
  return (
    <header
      data-section="header"
      className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col items-start gap-1 ${DONG_SECTION_H.header}`}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onBack}
        className="text-explore-xs text-text-brand font-medium"
      >
        {EXPLORE_COPY.backToGraph(dong.sigungu.name)}
      </button>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <h2 className="text-heading-xl text-text-primary leading-6">{dong.name}</h2>
        {coastal && (
          <span className="bg-explore-coast-bg text-explore-fact-coast text-explore-xs rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 font-medium">
            {EXPLORE_COPY.coastChip(dong.coastKm!)}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <p className="text-caption-xs text-text-secondary">
        {EXPLORE_COPY.dongSub(dong.sido, dong.sigungu.name)}
      </p>
    </header>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/TypeBars.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = { types: DongDetail['types'] }

/** ② 유형 막대 4개 (Figma 436:10461). 응답 순서 = 점수 높은 순, 첫째가 1위 */
export default function TypeBars({ types }: Props) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="explore-types"
      data-section="types"
      className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1.5 ${DONG_SECTION_H.types}`}
    >
      <h3 id="explore-types" className="text-explore-sm text-text-primary font-bold">
        {EXPLORE_COPY.typesTitle}
      </h3>
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {types.map((t, i) => {
          const top = i === 0
          return (
            <li key={t.name} data-top={top || undefined} className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
              <div className="flex h-4 items-center">
                <span
                  data-name
                  className={`text-caption-xs text-text-primary w-21 shrink-0 ${top ? 'font-bold' : 'font-medium'}`}
                >
                  {t.name}
                </span>
                <span className="bg-chart-bar-muted relative h-2 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-sm">
                  <span
                    data-fill
                    className={`absolute inset-y-0 left-0 rounded-sm ${top ? 'bg-brand-primary' : 'bg-chart-bar'}`}
                    style={{ width: `${t.score}%` }}
                  />
                </span>
                <span
                  className={`text-caption-xs w-11 shrink-0 text-right font-bold ${top ? 'text-text-brand' : 'text-text-primary'}`}
                >
                  {t.score}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-explore-2xs text-text-tertiary">{t.reason}</p>
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ul>
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = { sentences: string[] }

/** ② 이 동네에서 일하는 하루 (Figma 436:10587). 백엔드 3문장을 그대로 한 문단으로. 근거 칩·F 번호 없음 (스펙 §11) */
export default function Briefing({ sentences }: Props) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-labelledby="explore-briefing"
      data-section="briefing"
      className={`bg-bg-subtle flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-3 ${DONG_SECTION_H.briefing}`}
    >
      <h3 id="explore-briefing" className="text-explore-sm text-text-primary font-bold">
        {EXPLORE_COPY.briefingTitle}
      </h3>
      {sentences.length > 0 ? (
        <p className="text-caption-xs text-text-primary leading-normal">{sentences.join(' ')}</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-caption-xs text-text-secondary">{EXPLORE_COPY.briefingEmpty}</p>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```

### Task 15: HourRingGraph — 24시간 링 + 칩 + 평일·주말 토글

**Files:**

- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph.tsx`
- Test: `src/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph.test.tsx`

**Figma:** `436:10491` (EvidenceGraph 24시간 링 + 칩, 399×212)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes (A): `ringCells(hourly, day): { hour: number; value: number | null; intensity: number }[]`, `hourChipLabel(hourly, day): string | null`(값 없으면 null → 칩 숨김, 예 `도로 한산 · 평일 9시`), `monthChipLabel(months): string | null`(예 `한산한 달 · 2·4·9월`), `gradeChipLabel(key, g): string`(예 `저녁 활기 A / 62점`), 타입 `DayGroup`, `DetailKind`, `DongDetail`. 기존 `HEAT_VAR`, `colorScale` (`@/features/heatmap/colorScale`)
- Produces: `HourRingGraph(props: { dong: DongDetail; day: DayGroup; onDay: (d: DayGroup) => void; detail: DetailKind; onDetail: (k: DetailKind) => void })`
  - 루트 `data-section="graph"`, 등급 칩 `[data-chip="grade"]`(div, 버튼 아님), 시간대·한산한 달 칩은 `button[aria-pressed]`, 토글은 `role="tablist"` 이름 "평일·주말", 링 칸 `path[data-hour]`

- [ ] **Step 1: Figma 확인**

`get_design_context` 로 `436:10491` 을 본다. 배치는 절대 좌표 그대로 옮긴다(링 그래프는 고정 배치가 의도).

- 왼쪽 등급 칩 3개: x 0, y 22 / 88 / 154, 110×40, `bg/surface`, 테두리 1 `border/default`, 모서리 10(구현 `rounded-lg` 8), pl 10. 제목 11px Bold, 아래 10px `text/secondary`
- 오른쪽 칩: 시간대 y 52, 한산한 달 y 124. 선택 칩 = `bg/subtle` 바탕 + 1.5 `text/primary` 테두리(구현 `border-2`)
- 링: (134, 46) 132×132, 바깥 반지름 66 · 안 반지름 약 44, 24칸, 위 0시부터 시계방향. 가운데 동 이름 12px Bold + `평일 차량` 10px. 0·6·12·18 눈금 9px `text/tertiary`
- 선: 칩 안쪽 끝 → 링 중심(200, 112), `border/default`
- `이 동 하루 기준` 9.5px `text/tertiary` 링 아래 가운데 (y 192)
- 토글: 오른쪽 위 86×23, `bg/subtle` 알약 p 2, 선택 칸 `bg/inverse` + `text/inverse` 11px Bold, 나머지 11px `text/secondary`
- 링 색: Figma 는 분홍 단계. 구현은 `intensity`(48칸 최대 기준)를 히트맵 7단계 `HEAT_VAR[colorScale(intensity*100)]` 로 칠한다(스펙 §2 "색 진하기 = 이 동 안 최대값 기준"). 값 `null` 칸은 `chart/bar-muted` 회색

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`src/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph.test.tsx`

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import HourRingGraph from '@/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph'
import { gradeChipLabel, hourChipLabel, monthChipLabel } from '@/features/explore/labels'
import { JUMUNJIN } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

const setup = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof HourRingGraph>[0]> = {}) => {
  const props = {
    dong: JUMUNJIN,
    day: 'weekday' as const,
    onDay: vi.fn(),
    detail: 'hour' as const,
    onDetail: vi.fn(),
    ...over,
  }
  const view = render(<HourRingGraph {...props} />)
  return { ...view, props }
}

describe('HourRingGraph', () => {
  it('등급 칩 3개(등급 + 점수), 버튼이 아니고 눌러도 아무 일 없음', async () => {
    const { container, props } = setup()
    const grades = container.querySelectorAll('[data-chip="grade"]')
    expect(grades).toHaveLength(3)
    const evening = screen.getByLabelText(gradeChipLabel('evening', JUMUNJIN.grades.evening!))
    expect(evening.tagName).toBe('DIV')
    await userEvent.click(evening)
    expect(props.onDetail).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(props.onDay).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('값 없는 등급은 그 칩만 숨긴다', () => {
    const dong = { ...JUMUNJIN, grades: { ...JUMUNJIN.grades, peer: null } }
    const { container } = setup({ dong })
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-chip="grade"]')).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('처음엔 시간대 칩 선택, 한산한 달 칩을 누르면 onDetail(month)', async () => {
    const { props } = setup()
    const hour = screen.getByRole('button', { name: hourChipLabel(JUMUNJIN.hourly, 'weekday') })
    expect(hour).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true')
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: monthChipLabel(JUMUNJIN.months) }))
    expect(props.onDetail).toHaveBeenCalledWith('month')
  })

  it('토글 주말 → onDay(weekend). day=weekend 이면 링 색·시간대 칩·가운데 글자가 바뀐다', async () => {
    const { container, props, rerender } = setup()
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '주말' }))
    expect(props.onDay).toHaveBeenCalledWith('weekend')

    const fillAt = (h: number) =>
      container.querySelector(`path[data-hour="${h}"]`)!.getAttribute('fill')
    const weekday14 = fillAt(14)
    rerender(<HourRingGraph {...props} day="weekend" />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '주말' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true')
    expect(fillAt(14)).not.toBe(weekday14)
    expect(
      screen.getByRole('button', { name: hourChipLabel(JUMUNJIN.hourly, 'weekend') }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('주말 차량')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('링 24칸, 가운데 동 이름, "이 동 하루 기준"', () => {
    const { container } = setup()
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('path[data-hour]')).toHaveLength(24)
    expect(screen.getByText('주문진읍')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('이 동 하루 기준')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph.tsx`

```tsx
import { HEAT_VAR, colorScale } from '@/features/heatmap/colorScale'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { gradeChipLabel, hourChipLabel, monthChipLabel } from '@/features/explore/labels'
import { ringCells } from '@/features/explore/ring'
import type { DayGroup, DetailKind, DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = {
  dong: DongDetail
  day: DayGroup
  onDay: (day: DayGroup) => void
  detail: DetailKind
  onDetail: (kind: DetailKind) => void
}

// Figma 436:10491 (400×212) 좌표
const RING = 132
const R_OUT = 66
const R_IN = 44
const GAP_DEG = 1.2
const CENTER = { x: 200, y: 112 }
const CHIP_W = 110
const CHIP_H = 40
const GRADE_SLOTS = [
  { key: 'quiet', top: 22 },
  { key: 'peer', top: 88 },
  { key: 'evening', top: 154 },
] as const
const HOUR_TOP = 52
const MONTH_TOP = 124
const DAYS: DayGroup[] = ['weekday', 'weekend']
const TICKS = [
  { label: '0', x: RING / 2, y: -4 },
  { label: '6', x: RING + 7, y: RING / 2 + 3 },
  { label: '12', x: RING / 2, y: RING + 10 },
  { label: '18', x: -8, y: RING / 2 + 3 },
]

function point(r: number, deg: number): string {
  const a = ((deg - 90) * Math.PI) / 180
  return `${(RING / 2 + r * Math.cos(a)).toFixed(2)} ${(RING / 2 + r * Math.sin(a)).toFixed(2)}`
}

/** hour 칸의 도넛 조각 (위 0시부터 시계방향 15°씩) */
function ringPath(hour: number): string {
  const a0 = hour * 15 + GAP_DEG / 2
  const a1 = (hour + 1) * 15 - GAP_DEG / 2
  return `M ${point(R_OUT, a0)} A ${R_OUT} ${R_OUT} 0 0 1 ${point(R_OUT, a1)} L ${point(R_IN, a1)} A ${R_IN} ${R_IN} 0 0 0 ${point(R_IN, a0)} Z`
}

/** '도로 한산 · 평일 9시' → ['도로 한산', '평일 9시'] (칩 두 줄) */
function splitAt(label: string, sep: string): [string, string] {
  const i = label.indexOf(sep)
  return i < 0 ? [label, ''] : [label.slice(0, i), label.slice(i + sep.length)]
}

const CHIP =
  'absolute flex h-10 w-27.5 flex-col items-start justify-center gap-px rounded-lg pl-2.5 text-left'

function ChipText({ label, sep }: { label: string; sep: string }) {
  const [title, sub] = splitAt(label, sep)
  return (
    <>
      <span className="text-explore-xs text-text-primary font-bold">{title}</span>
      <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary">{sub}</span>
    </>
  )
}

/** ② 그래프 (Figma 436:10491): 24시간 링 + 등급·시간대·한산한 달 칩 + 평일·주말 토글 (스펙 §3-3) */
export default function HourRingGraph({ dong, day, onDay, detail, onDetail }: Props) {
  const cells = ringCells(dong.hourly, day)
  const dayLabel = EXPLORE_COPY.dayLabel[day]
  const grades = GRADE_SLOTS.flatMap((slot) => {
    const g = dong.grades[slot.key]
    return g ? [{ ...slot, label: gradeChipLabel(slot.key, g) }] : []
  })
  const hourLabel = hourChipLabel(dong.hourly, day)
  const monthLabel = monthChipLabel(dong.months)
  // 값이 없으면 문구 함수가 null → 그 칩을 숨긴다 (Task 5)
  const selectable = [
    { kind: 'hour' as const, top: HOUR_TOP, label: hourLabel },
    { kind: 'month' as const, top: MONTH_TOP, label: monthLabel },
  ].flatMap((c) => (c.label === null ? [] : [{ ...c, label: c.label }]))

  return (
    <section
      aria-label={EXPLORE_COPY.ringLabel(dayLabel)}
      data-section="graph"
      className={`relative w-full shrink-0 ${DONG_SECTION_H.graph}`}
    >
      <svg
        aria-hidden
        viewBox="0 0 400 212"
        preserveAspectRatio="none"
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 size-full"
      >
        {grades.map((g) => (
          <line
            key={g.key}
            x1={CHIP_W}
            y1={g.top + CHIP_H / 2}
            x2={CENTER.x}
            y2={CENTER.y}
            className="stroke-border-default"
          />
        ))}
        {selectable.map((c) => (
          <line
            key={c.kind}
            x1={400 - CHIP_W}
            y1={c.top + CHIP_H / 2}
            x2={CENTER.x}
            y2={CENTER.y}
            className={detail === c.kind ? 'stroke-text-primary' : 'stroke-border-default'}
            strokeWidth={detail === c.kind ? 1.5 : 1}
          />
        ))}
      </svg>

      <div className="absolute top-11.5 left-1/2 size-33 -translate-x-1/2">
        <svg aria-hidden viewBox={`0 0 ${RING} ${RING}`} className="size-full overflow-visible">
          <circle cx={RING / 2} cy={RING / 2} r={R_IN} className="fill-bg-surface" />
          {cells.map((c) => (
            <path
              key={c.hour}
              d={ringPath(c.hour)}
              data-hour={c.hour}
              fill={
                c.value === null
                  ? 'var(--color-chart-bar-muted)'
                  : `var(${HEAT_VAR[colorScale(c.intensity * 100)]})`
              }
            />
          ))}
          {TICKS.map((t) => (
            <text
              key={t.label}
              x={t.x}
              y={t.y}
              textAnchor="middle"
              className="fill-text-tertiary text-explore-3xs"
            >
              {t.label}
            </text>
          ))}
        </svg>
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          <span className="text-caption-xs text-text-primary font-bold">{dong.name}</span>
          <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary">
            {EXPLORE_COPY.ringCenterSub(dayLabel)}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="text-explore-3xs text-text-tertiary absolute top-48 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2">
        {EXPLORE_COPY.ringCaption}
      </p>

      {grades.map((g) => (
        <div
          key={g.key}
          data-chip="grade"
          aria-label={g.label}
          className={`${CHIP} bg-bg-surface border-border-default left-0 cursor-default border`}
          style={{ top: g.top }}
        >
          <ChipText label={g.label} sep=" / " />
        </div>
      ))}

      {selectable.map((c) => {
        const on = detail === c.kind
        return (
          <button
            key={c.kind}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={on}
            aria-label={c.label}
            onClick={() => onDetail(c.kind)}
            className={`${CHIP} right-0 ${on ? 'bg-bg-subtle border-text-primary border-2' : 'bg-bg-surface border-border-default border'}`}
            style={{ top: c.top }}
          >
            <ChipText label={c.label} sep=" · " />
          </button>
        )
      })}

      <div
        role="tablist"
        aria-label={EXPLORE_COPY.dayToggleLabel}
        className="bg-bg-subtle absolute top-0 right-0 inline-flex rounded-full p-0.5"
      >
        {DAYS.map((d) => {
          const on = d === day
          return (
            <button
              key={d}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={on}
              onClick={() => onDay(d)}
              className={`text-explore-xs rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 ${on ? 'bg-bg-inverse text-text-inverse font-bold' : 'text-text-secondary'}`}
            >
              {EXPLORE_COPY.dayLabel[d]}
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

### Task 16: DetailSlot — 고정 높이 상세 칸 (HourBars / MonthBars)

**Files:**

- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/HourBars.tsx`
- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/MonthBars.tsx`
- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot.tsx`
- Test: `src/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot.test.tsx`

**Figma:** `436:10552` (DetailSlot 고정 높이 · 시간대 선택, 400×112). 12개월 막대는 Figma 에 없음 — 같은 틀(머리 줄 + 막대 64)에 12칸으로 그린다

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `DongDetail`, `DayGroup`, `DetailKind` (A), `DONG_SECTION_H`, `EXPLORE_COPY` (13)
- Produces:
  - `HourBars({ hourly: DongDetail['hourly']; day: DayGroup })` — 막대 `[data-hour]` + `data-tone="quiet" | "peak" | "base" | "empty"`
  - `MonthBars({ months: DongDetail['months'] })` — 막대 `[data-month]` + `data-tone="quiet" | "base" | "empty"`
  - `DetailSlot({ detail: DetailKind; day: DayGroup; dong: DongDetail })` — 루트 `data-section="detail"` + `data-testid="detail-slot"`

- [ ] **Step 1: Figma 확인**

`get_design_context` 로 `436:10552` 를 본다. `bg/surface`, 테두리 1 `border/default`, 모서리 12(구현 `rounded-xl`), pt 10 · pb 8 · px 12, gap 6. 머리: 왼쪽 11.5px Bold(구현 12px `text-caption-xs`), 오른쪽 10px `text/secondary`. 막대 영역 64(막대 최대 50 + 라벨 11), 막대 폭 12.5 · 간격 약 3.3, 위쪽 모서리 2. 색: 기본 `chart/bar-muted`, 한산한 시각 `brand/primary`, 가장 붐비는 시각 `text/secondary`. 라벨 9px `text/tertiary`, 한산한 시각 라벨은 Bold `text/primary`. 막대 높이는 그 요일의 최대값 기준(Figma 12시 = 50).

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`src/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot.test.tsx`

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import DetailSlot from '@/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot'
import HourBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/HourBars'
import MonthBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/MonthBars'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'
import { JUMUNJIN } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

const tone = (c: HTMLElement, sel: string) => c.querySelector(sel)?.getAttribute('data-tone')

describe('HourBars', () => {
  it('평일: 9시 한산 강조, 12시 붐빔 표시, 요약 문구', () => {
    const { container } = render(<HourBars hourly={JUMUNJIN.hourly} day="weekday" />)
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-hour]')).toHaveLength(24)
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="9"]')).toBe('quiet')
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="12"]')).toBe('peak')
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="0"]')).toBe('base')
    expect(screen.getByText('평일 시간대별 차량 이동')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('9시 가장 한산 · 12시 가장 많음 (2,064명)')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
  it('주말: 3시 한산, 14시 붐빔', () => {
    const { container } = render(<HourBars hourly={JUMUNJIN.hourly} day="weekend" />)
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="3"]')).toBe('quiet')
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="14"]')).toBe('peak')
    expect(screen.getByText('3시 가장 한산 · 14시 가장 많음 (2,833명)')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
  it('값 없는 시각은 회색 빈 막대', () => {
    const weekday = {
      ...JUMUNJIN.hourly.weekday,
      driv: JUMUNJIN.hourly.weekday.driv.map((v, h) => (h === 2 ? null : v)),
    }
    const { container } = render(
      <HourBars hourly={{ ...JUMUNJIN.hourly, weekday }} day="weekday" />,
    )
    expect(tone(container, '[data-hour="2"]')).toBe('empty')
  })
})

describe('MonthBars', () => {
  it('12개월, 한산한 3개월 강조, "강릉시 기준", null 달은 빈 막대', () => {
    const months = {
      ...JUMUNJIN.months,
      raw: JUMUNJIN.months.raw.map((v, i) => (i === 10 ? null : v)),
    }
    const { container } = render(<MonthBars months={months} />)
    expect(container.querySelectorAll('[data-month]')).toHaveLength(12)
    expect(['2', '4', '9'].map((m) => tone(container, `[data-month="${m}"]`))).toEqual([
      'quiet',
      'quiet',
      'quiet',
    ])
    expect(tone(container, '[data-month="8"]')).toBe('base')
    expect(tone(container, '[data-month="11"]')).toBe('empty')
    expect(screen.getByText('강릉시 기준')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})

describe('DetailSlot', () => {
  it('시간대 ↔ 한산한 달로 바꿔도 칸 높이 클래스가 같다', () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<DetailSlot detail="hour" day="weekday" dong={JUMUNJIN} />)
    const slot = screen.getByTestId('detail-slot')
    expect(slot).toHaveClass(DONG_SECTION_H.detail)
    expect(screen.getByText('평일 시간대별 차량 이동')).toBeInTheDocument()
    rerender(<DetailSlot detail="month" day="weekday" dong={JUMUNJIN} />)
    expect(screen.getByTestId('detail-slot')).toBe(slot)
    expect(slot).toHaveClass(DONG_SECTION_H.detail)
    expect(screen.getByText('강릉시 기준')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByText('평일 시간대별 차량 이동')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/HourBars.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DayGroup, DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'

type Props = { hourly: DongDetail['hourly']; day: DayGroup }

const LABEL_HOURS = new Set([0, 6, 12, 18, 23])
const TONE_CLASS = {
  quiet: 'bg-brand-primary',
  peak: 'bg-text-secondary',
  base: 'bg-chart-bar-muted',
  empty: 'bg-bg-surface-strong h-full',
} as const

/** 상세 칸 · 24시간 차량 막대 (Figma 436:10556). 높이는 그 요일 최대값 기준 */
export default function HourBars({ hourly, day }: Props) {
  const { driv, peakHour, quietHour } = hourly[day]
  const max = Math.max(1, ...driv.map((v) => v ?? 0))
  const peak = peakHour === null ? null : driv[peakHour]
  const dayLabel = EXPLORE_COPY.dayLabel[day]
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <span className="text-caption-xs text-text-primary font-bold">
          {EXPLORE_COPY.hourBarsTitle(dayLabel)}
        </span>
        {quietHour !== null && peakHour !== null && peak !== null && peak !== undefined && (
          <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary">
            {EXPLORE_COPY.hourBarsSummary(quietHour, peakHour, peak)}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="flex h-16 gap-0.75">
        {driv.map((v, h) => {
          const tone =
            v === null ? 'empty' : h === quietHour ? 'quiet' : h === peakHour ? 'peak' : 'base'
          const showLabel = LABEL_HOURS.has(h) || h === quietHour
          return (
            <div key={h} className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-0.75">
              <div className="flex h-12.5 w-full items-end">
                <div
                  data-hour={h}
                  data-tone={tone}
                  title={
                    v === null ? EXPLORE_COPY.noValue : `${h}시 ${v.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}명`
                  }
                  className={`w-full rounded-t-sm ${TONE_CLASS[tone]}`}
                  style={v === null ? undefined : { height: `${Math.max(2, (v / max) * 100)}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <span
                className={`text-explore-3xs h-2.75 whitespace-nowrap ${h === quietHour ? 'text-text-primary font-bold' : 'text-text-tertiary'}`}
              >
                {showLabel ? EXPLORE_COPY.hourUnit(h) : ''}
              </span>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/MonthBars.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'

type Props = { months: DongDetail['months'] }

const TONE_CLASS = {
  quiet: 'bg-brand-primary',
  base: 'bg-chart-bar-muted',
  empty: 'border-border-default h-full border border-dashed',
} as const

/** 상세 칸 · 12개월 막대 (months.raw = 시군구 ease 원값 0~100). 한산한 3개월 강조, null 달은 빈 막대 (스펙 §3-3) */
export default function MonthBars({ months }: Props) {
  const quiet = new Set(months.quiet)
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <span className="text-caption-xs text-text-primary font-bold">
          {EXPLORE_COPY.monthBarsTitle}
        </span>
        <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary">
          {EXPLORE_COPY.monthBasis(months.basis)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex h-16 gap-1.5">
        {months.raw.map((v, i) => {
          const m = i + 1
          const tone = v === null ? 'empty' : quiet.has(m) ? 'quiet' : 'base'
          return (
            <div key={m} className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-0.75">
              <div className="flex h-12.5 w-full items-end">
                <div
                  data-month={m}
                  data-tone={tone}
                  title={v === null ? EXPLORE_COPY.noValue : `${m}월 ${Math.round(v)}`}
                  className={`w-full rounded-t-sm ${TONE_CLASS[tone]}`}
                  style={v === null ? undefined : { height: `${Math.max(2, v)}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <span
                className={`text-explore-3xs h-2.75 whitespace-nowrap ${quiet.has(m) ? 'text-text-primary font-bold' : 'text-text-tertiary'}`}
              >
                {EXPLORE_COPY.monthUnit(m)}
              </span>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DayGroup, DetailKind, DongDetail } from '@/features/explore/types'
import HourBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/HourBars'
import MonthBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/MonthBars'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = { detail: DetailKind; day: DayGroup; dong: DongDetail }

/**
 * ② 상세 칸 (Figma 436:10552). 높이 고정, 내용만 바꾸고 150ms 페이드 (스펙 §4).
 * key 가 바뀌면 안쪽만 다시 그려져 페이드가 다시 돈다
 */
export default function DetailSlot({ detail, day, dong }: Props) {
  return (
    <section
      aria-label={EXPLORE_COPY.detailLabel}
      data-section="detail"
      data-testid="detail-slot"
      className={`border-border-default bg-bg-surface shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border px-3 pt-2.5 pb-2 ${DONG_SECTION_H.detail}`}
    >
      <div key={detail === 'hour' ? `hour-${day}` : 'month'} className="animate-explore-fade">
        {detail === 'hour' ? (
          <HourBars hourly={dong.hourly} day={day} />
        ) : (
          <MonthBars months={dong.months} />
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import SimilarList from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SimilarList'
import { diffLabel } from '@/features/explore/labels'
import { GEUMAM, JUMUNJIN } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

describe('SimilarList', () => {
  it('바닷가 동: 제목·설명, 3행(이름 · 공통 n · 공통 사실 · 점수 차), 행 클릭 → onPick', async () => {
    const onPick = vi.fn()
    render(<SimilarList similar={JUMUNJIN.similar} coastal onPick={onPick} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '비슷한 바닷가 동' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('지표 거리 기준 · 점수 차 = 상대 − 이 동')).toBeInTheDocument()
    const rows = within(screen.getByRole('list', { name: '비슷한 바닷가 동' })).getAllByRole(
      'button',
    )
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('속초시 노학동')
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('공통 6')
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent('조용함 B · 1위 장기체류형 · 바닷가')
    expect(rows[0]).toHaveTextContent(diffLabel(JUMUNJIN.similar[0].diff))
    await userEvent.click(rows[1])
    expect(onPick).toHaveBeenCalledWith(JUMUNJIN.similar[1])
  })
  it('내륙 동은 "비슷한 동"', () => {
    render(<SimilarList similar={GEUMAM.similar} coastal={false} onPick={() => {}} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: '비슷한 동' })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
  it('빈 배열이면 칸을 숨긴다', () => {
    const { container } = render(<SimilarList similar={[]} coastal onPick={() => {}} />)
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 구현**

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SimilarList.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { diffLabel } from '@/features/explore/labels'
import type { SimilarDong } from '@/features/explore/types'

type Props = { similar: SimilarDong[]; coastal: boolean; onPick: (similar: SimilarDong) => void }

/** ② 비슷한 동 (Figma 436:10590). 백엔드가 고른 순서 그대로, 공통 사실은 드문 것부터 최대 3개 (스펙 §3-3) */
export default function SimilarList({ similar, coastal, onPick }: Props) {
  if (similar.length === 0) return null
  const title = coastal ? EXPLORE_COPY.similarCoastTitle : EXPLORE_COPY.similarPlainTitle
  return (
    <section className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1.5">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <h3 id="explore-similar" className="text-explore-sm text-text-primary font-bold">
          {title}
        </h3>
        <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-tertiary">{EXPLORE_COPY.similarHint}</span>
      </div>
      <ul aria-labelledby="explore-similar" className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {similar.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onPick(s)}
              className="hover:bg-bg-subtle flex w-full flex-col items-start gap-px rounded-sm text-left"
            >
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-caption-xs text-text-primary font-medium">{`${s.sigungu} ${s.name}`}</span>
                <span className="bg-bg-subtle text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary rounded-full px-1.5 py-px font-bold">
                  {EXPLORE_COPY.cardCommon(s.common)}
                </span>
                <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-secondary">
                  {s.commonTop.join(' · ')}
                </span>
              </span>
              <span className="text-explore-2xs text-text-tertiary">{diffLabel(s.diff)}</span>
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  )
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes (A): `useDongQuery(id: string | null)` (UseQueryResult<DongDetail>), `useDelayedLoading(loading, delay = 200, min = 400)`, `fetchDong` (`@/features/explore/api`, 테스트에서 vi.mock), `NotFoundError` (`@/lib/errors`). 13~17 컴포넌트 전부
- Produces:
  - `PanelMessage({ message: string; actionLabel: string; onAction: () => void })` — `role="alert"`
  - `PanelSkeleton()` — `data-testid="panel-skeleton"`, `aria-busy="true"`, 섹션마다 `data-section`(header·types·graph·detail·briefing)
  - `DongPanel({ dongId: string; onBack: (dongId: string) => void; onClose: () => void; onOpenSimilar: (s: SimilarDong) => void })` — 평일·주말(`day`)과 상세 칸(`detail`)은 동마다 처음 값(평일·시간대)으로 시작

- [ ] **Step 1: Figma 확인**

`get_design_context` 로 `436:10279` 와 `429:10607` 을 본다.

- 실제 패널: 위 여백 16, 섹션 간격 12 (헤더 16→75, 유형 87, 그래프 263, 상세 487, 브리핑 611, 비슷한 동 723, 출처 866)
- 로딩(L3): 헤더 막대 120×24 · 200×14 / 유형 제목 90×14 + 4줄(70×14 이름, 250·200·170·140×10 막대, 24×14 점수) / 회색 도넛 128(구멍 80) + 칩 자리 5개 + 토글 자리 100×24 / 상세 칸 상자 / 문장 줄(150×14 + 369·300·369·220×12) / 비슷한 동 3행(90·160·91×14) / 출처 260×10
- **L3 의 섹션 높이(그래프 240, 상세 104, 브리핑 140)는 ②-v6 이전 배치 기준이다.** 스펙 §9-1 "실제 화면과 같은 자리·크기"에 따라 높이는 `DONG_SECTION_H`(②-v6 값)를 쓰고, 블록 모양만 L3 을 따른다

`src/features/explore/components/panel/DongPanel.test.tsx`

```tsx
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import renderWithRouter from '@/test/renderWithRouter'
import { GEUMAM, JUMUNJIN } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

vi.mock('@/features/explore/api', () => ({ fetchDong: vi.fn(), fetchPins: vi.fn() }))

import { fetchDong } from '@/features/explore/api'
import DongPanel from '@/features/explore/components/panel/DongPanel'
import PanelSkeleton from '@/features/explore/components/panel/PanelSkeleton'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'
import { hourChipLabel } from '@/features/explore/labels'
import { NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors'

const mocked = vi.mocked(fetchDong)
const props = () => ({ onBack: vi.fn(), onClose: vi.fn(), onOpenSimilar: vi.fn() })

beforeEach(() => mocked.mockReset())

describe('DongPanel', () => {
  it('성공: 헤더 → 유형 → 그래프 → 상세 → 브리핑 → 비슷한 동 → 출처', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(JUMUNJIN)
    const p = props()
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...p} />)
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: '워케이션 유형' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(JUMUNJIN.briefing.join(' '))).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByRole('list', { name: '비슷한 바닷가 동' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('데이터: LG U+ 유동인구 2025 · 갱신 2026-11-13')).toBeInTheDocument()
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '← 강릉시 그래프' }))
    expect(p.onBack).toHaveBeenCalledWith(JUMUNJIN.id)
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /속초시 노학동/ }))
    expect(p.onOpenSimilar).toHaveBeenCalledWith(JUMUNJIN.similar[0])
  })

  it('토글 하나로 링·시간대 칩·24시간 막대가 함께 바뀐다', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(JUMUNJIN)
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: '주말' }))
    expect(
      screen.getByRole('button', { name: hourChipLabel(JUMUNJIN.hourly, 'weekend') }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('주말 시간대별 차량 이동')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('주말 차량')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('한산한 달 칩 → 상세 칸만 바뀌고 높이 클래스는 같다', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(JUMUNJIN)
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /^한산한 달/ }))
    expect(screen.getByTestId('detail-slot')).toHaveClass(DONG_SECTION_H.detail)
    expect(within(screen.getByTestId('detail-slot')).getByText('강릉시 기준')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('브리핑 없음 → 준비 중, 비슷한 동 없음 → 칸 숨김', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue({ ...JUMUNJIN, briefing: [], similar: [] })
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    expect(await screen.findByText('브리핑을 준비하고 있어요.')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('heading', { name: '비슷한 바닷가 동' })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('내륙 동은 "비슷한 동"', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(GEUMAM)
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={GEUMAM.id} {...props()} />)
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: '비슷한 동' })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('503 → "그래프를 불러오지 못했어요." + 다시 시도', async () => {
    mocked.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('API 503 Service Unavailable: /api/explore/dongs/x'))
    mocked.mockResolvedValueOnce(JUMUNJIN)
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    const alert = await screen.findByRole('alert')
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('그래프를 불러오지 못했어요.')
    await userEvent.click(within(alert).getByRole('button', { name: '다시 시도' }))
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(mocked).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
  })

  it('404 → "동네 정보를 찾을 수 없어요." + 패널 닫기', async () => {
    mocked.mockRejectedValue(new NotFoundError('/api/explore/dongs/0000000000'))
    const p = props()
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId="0000000000" {...p} />)
    const alert = await screen.findByRole('alert')
    expect(alert).toHaveTextContent('동네 정보를 찾을 수 없어요.')
    await userEvent.click(within(alert).getByRole('button', { name: '패널 닫기' }))
    expect(p.onClose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce()
  })

  it('0.2초 안에 오면 스켈레톤을 띄우지 않는다', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(JUMUNJIN)
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('panel-skeleton')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })
    expect(screen.queryByTestId('panel-skeleton')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('느리면 0.2초 뒤 스켈레톤(aria-busy + 숨김 문구)', async () => {
    mocked.mockReturnValue(new Promise(() => {}))
    renderWithRouter(<DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />)
    const sk = await screen.findByTestId('panel-skeleton', {}, { timeout: 1000 })
    expect(sk).toHaveAttribute('aria-busy', 'true')
    expect(within(sk).getByText('불러오는 중')).toHaveClass('sr-only')
  })

  it('스켈레톤과 실제 패널의 섹션 높이가 같다', async () => {
    mocked.mockResolvedValue(JUMUNJIN)
    const { container } = renderWithRouter(
      <>
        <div data-testid="real">
          <DongPanel dongId={JUMUNJIN.id} {...props()} />
        </div>
        <div data-testid="sk">
          <PanelSkeleton />
        </div>
      </>,
    )
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 2, name: '주문진읍' })
    for (const key of Object.keys(DONG_SECTION_H) as (keyof typeof DONG_SECTION_H)[]) {
      const real = container.querySelector(`[data-testid="real"] [data-section="${key}"]`)
      const sk = container.querySelector(`[data-testid="sk"] [data-section="${key}"]`)
      expect(real, key).toHaveClass(DONG_SECTION_H[key])
      expect(sk, key).toHaveClass(DONG_SECTION_H[key])
    }
  })
})
```

```tsx
type Props = { message: string; actionLabel: string; onAction: () => void }

/** 패널 오류·404 (스펙 §9). 문구 + 버튼 하나 */
export default function PanelMessage({ message, actionLabel, onAction }: Props) {
  return (
    <div role="alert" className="flex flex-col items-start gap-3 pt-5">
      <p className="text-body-base text-text-primary font-medium">{message}</p>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onAction}
        className="bg-selected-bg text-selected-fg text-body-sm rounded-lg px-4 py-2 font-bold"
      >
        {actionLabel}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/PanelSkeleton.tsx`

```tsx
import Block from '@/features/explore/components/Skeleton'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { DONG_SECTION_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

const TYPE_BARS = ['w-62.5', 'w-50', 'w-42.5', 'w-35']
const BRIEF_LINES = ['w-full', 'w-75', 'w-full', 'w-55']

/** ② 로딩 (Figma L3 429:10607). 블록 모양은 L3, 섹션 높이는 실제 패널과 같은 DONG_SECTION_H */
export default function PanelSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div data-testid="panel-skeleton" aria-busy="true" className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 pt-4">
      <span className="sr-only">{EXPLORE_COPY.loadingSr}</span>

      <div
        data-section="header"
        className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col justify-center gap-2 ${DONG_SECTION_H.header}`}
      >
        <Block className="h-6 w-30" />
        <Block className="h-3.5 w-50" />
      </div>

      <div data-section="types" className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-3 ${DONG_SECTION_H.types}`}>
        <Block className="h-3.5 w-22.5" />
        {TYPE_BARS.map((w) => (
          <div key={w} className="flex items-center gap-5">
            <Block className="h-3.5 w-17.5" />
            <Block className={`h-2.5 ${w}`} />
            <Block className="ml-auto h-3.5 w-6" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div data-section="graph" className={`relative shrink-0 ${DONG_SECTION_H.graph}`}>
        <Block shape="circle" className="absolute top-11.5 left-1/2 size-33 -translate-x-1/2" />
        <div className="bg-bg-surface absolute top-24.5 left-1/2 size-20 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full" />
        <Block shape="pill" className="absolute top-0 right-0 h-6 w-21.5" />
        <Block className="absolute top-5.5 left-0 h-10 w-27.5 rounded-lg" />
        <Block className="absolute top-22 left-0 h-10 w-27.5 rounded-lg" />
        <Block className="absolute top-38.5 left-0 h-10 w-27.5 rounded-lg" />
        <Block className="absolute top-13 right-0 h-10 w-27.5 rounded-lg" />
        <Block className="absolute top-31 right-0 h-10 w-27.5 rounded-lg" />
      </div>

      <div data-section="detail" className={`shrink-0 ${DONG_SECTION_H.detail}`}>
        <Block className="size-full rounded-xl" />
      </div>

      <div
        data-section="briefing"
        className={`bg-bg-subtle flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-3 ${DONG_SECTION_H.briefing}`}
      >
        <Block className="h-3.5 w-37.5" />
        {BRIEF_LINES.map((w, i) => (
          <Block key={i} className={`h-3 ${w}`} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-3">
        <Block className="h-3.5 w-30" />
        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex gap-5">
            <Block className="h-3.5 w-22.5" />
            <Block className="h-3.5 w-40" />
            <Block className="ml-auto h-3.5 w-22.75" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Block className="mt-auto mb-4 h-2.5 w-65" />
    </div>
  )
}
```

(`Block` 의 `className` 에 `rounded-lg`·`rounded-xl` 을 주는 곳은 `shape="box"` 의 `rounded-sm` 과 겹친다. Tailwind v4 는 같은 속성 유틸이 겹치면 CSS 순서로 정해져 `rounded-xl` 이 이기지만, 헷갈리면 `Block` 에 `shape` 값 `'card'`(rounded-lg)·`'slot'`(rounded-xl)을 더해도 된다 — 이 경우 13 테스트에 한 줄 추가.)

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { DayGroup, DetailKind, DongDetail, SimilarDong } from '@/features/explore/types'
import { useDelayedLoading } from '@/features/explore/useDelayedLoading'
import { useDongQuery } from '@/features/explore/useDongQuery'
import { NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors'
import Briefing from '@/features/explore/components/panel/Briefing'
import DetailSlot from '@/features/explore/components/panel/DetailSlot'
import HourRingGraph from '@/features/explore/components/panel/HourRingGraph'
import PanelHeader from '@/features/explore/components/panel/PanelHeader'
import PanelMessage from '@/features/explore/components/panel/PanelMessage'
import PanelSkeleton from '@/features/explore/components/panel/PanelSkeleton'
import SimilarList from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SimilarList'
import SourceLine from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SourceLine'
import TypeBars from '@/features/explore/components/panel/TypeBars'

type Props = {
  dongId: string
  onBack: (dongId: string) => void
  onClose: () => void
  onOpenSimilar: (similar: SimilarDong) => void
}

/** ② 동 패널 (Figma 436:10279). 로딩 0.2초 뒤 스켈레톤(최소 0.4초), 503·404 분기 (스펙 §9) */
export default function DongPanel({ dongId, onBack, onClose, onOpenSimilar }: Props) {
  const q = useDongQuery(dongId)
  const skeleton = useDelayedLoading(q.isPending)

  if (skeleton) return <PanelSkeleton />
  if (q.isPending) {
    return (
      <div aria-busy="true" className="flex-1">
        <span className="sr-only">{EXPLORE_COPY.loadingSr}</span>
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (q.isError) {
    return q.error instanceof NotFoundError ? (
      <PanelMessage
        message={EXPLORE_COPY.dongNotFound}
        actionLabel={EXPLORE_COPY.closePanel}
        onAction={onClose}
      />
    ) : (
      <PanelMessage
        message={EXPLORE_COPY.dongError}
        actionLabel={EXPLORE_COPY.retry}
        onAction={() => q.refetch()}
      />
    )
  }
  return (
    <DongBody
      key={q.data.id}
      dong={q.data}
      onBack={() => onBack(q.data.id)}
      onOpenSimilar={onOpenSimilar}
    />
  )
}

type BodyProps = {
  dong: DongDetail
  onBack: () => void
  onOpenSimilar: (similar: SimilarDong) => void
}

/** 평일·주말과 상세 칸은 URL 에 넣지 않는다. 동이 바뀌면 key 로 다시 만들어 평일·시간대로 돌아간다 (스펙 §5) */
function DongBody({ dong, onBack, onOpenSimilar }: BodyProps) {
  const [day, setDay] = useState<DayGroup>('weekday')
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<DetailKind>('hour')
  return (
    <article aria-label={dong.name} className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3 pt-4">
      <PanelHeader dong={dong} onBack={onBack} />
      <TypeBars types={dong.types} />
      <HourRingGraph dong={dong} day={day} onDay={setDay} detail={detail} onDetail={setDetail} />
      <DetailSlot dong={dong} day={day} detail={detail} />
      <Briefing sentences={dong.briefing} />
      <SimilarList
        similar={dong.similar}
        coastal={dong.coastKm !== null && dong.coastKm <= 1}
        onPick={onOpenSimilar}
      />
      <SourceLine>{EXPLORE_COPY.dongSource(dong.syncedAt.slice(0, 10))}</SourceLine>
    </article>
  )
}
```

(`useDelayedLoading`·`useDongQuery` 의 파일 경로는 파트 A 가 만든 경로로 맞춘다. 이 계획은 `src/features/explore/useDelayedLoading.ts`, `src/features/explore/useDongQuery.ts` 로 가정.)

**Interfaces:**

  - `type LayoutNode = SimulationNodeDatum & { id: string; r: number; x: number; y: number }`
  - `createLayout(graph, w, h): { nodes: LayoutNode[]; byId: Map<string, LayoutNode>; sim: Simulation<LayoutNode, undefined> }` — 300틱을 미리 돌린 결과(같은 데이터 = 같은 모양)

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { FACT_R, createLayout, nodeRadius } from '@/features/explore/forceLayout'
import { RELATIONS, buildSigunguGraph } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { GANGNEUNG_PINS } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

const W = 399
const H = 400
const on = new Set(RELATIONS.filter((r) => r.defaultOn).map((r) => r.key))
const graph = buildSigunguGraph(GANGNEUNG_PINS, on)

describe('nodeRadius', () => {
  it('동은 1위 점수에 비례(Figma: 61 → 지름 14, 97 → 22), 사실은 고정 26', () => {
    expect(nodeRadius({ id: 'a', kind: 'dong', label: 'a', score: 61 })).toBeCloseTo(6.95, 1)
    expect(nodeRadius({ id: 'a', kind: 'dong', label: 'a', score: 97 })).toBeCloseTo(11.06, 1)
    expect(nodeRadius({ id: 'a', kind: 'dong', label: 'a', score: 10 })).toBe(6)
    expect(nodeRadius({ id: 'coast', kind: 'coast', label: '바닷가' })).toBe(FACT_R)
  })
})

describe('createLayout', () => {
  it('같은 데이터면 같은 위치 (씨앗 고정)', () => {
    const a = createLayout(graph, W, H).nodes.map((n) => [n.id, Math.round(n.x), Math.round(n.y)])
    const b = createLayout(graph, W, H).nodes.map((n) => [n.id, Math.round(n.x), Math.round(n.y)])
    expect(a).toEqual(b)
  })
  it('모든 노드가 영역 안 (반지름 + 아래 이름 칸)', () => {
    const { nodes } = createLayout(graph, W, H)
    expect(nodes).toHaveLength(graph.nodes.length)
    for (const n of nodes) {
      expect(n.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(n.r)
      expect(n.x).toBeLessThanOrEqual(W - n.r)
      expect(n.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(n.r)
      expect(n.y).toBeLessThanOrEqual(H - n.r - 14)
    }
  })
  it('원본 graph.links 를 바꾸지 않는다 (d3 가 source/target 을 객체로 바꾸는 것 방지)', () => {
    createLayout(graph, W, H)
    expect(typeof graph.links[0].source).toBe('string')
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패 확인**

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`src/features/explore/forceLayout.ts`

```ts
import { useEffect, useMemo, useReducer } from 'react'
import { forceCollide, forceLink, forceManyBody, forceSimulation, forceX, forceY } from 'd3-force'
import type { Simulation, SimulationLinkDatum, SimulationNodeDatum } from 'd3-force'
import { seedPosition } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { GraphNode, SigunguGraphData } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'

/** 사실 노드 반지름 (Figma 434:9801 등 52×52) */
export const FACT_R = 26
/** 동 노드 아래 이름 칸 */
const LABEL_SPACE = 14
const TICKS = 300

/** 동: 1위 유형 점수 × 0.114 (Figma 61 → 지름 14, 97 → 22), 6~12 로 자름. 사실: 고정 */
export function nodeRadius(n: GraphNode): number {
  if (n.kind !== 'dong') return FACT_R
  return Math.min(12, Math.max(6, (n.score ?? 50) * 0.114))
}

export type LayoutNode = SimulationNodeDatum & { id: string; r: number; x: number; y: number }
export type Layout = {
  nodes: LayoutNode[]
  byId: Map<string, LayoutNode>
  sim: Simulation<LayoutNode, SimulationLinkDatum<LayoutNode>>
}

function clamp(nodes: LayoutNode[], w: number, h: number) {
  for (const n of nodes) {
    n.x = Math.min(w - n.r, Math.max(n.r, n.x))
    n.y = Math.min(h - n.r - LABEL_SPACE, Math.max(n.r, n.y))
  }
}

/**
 * 스펙 §7 힘 값: manyBody −260, link 80, collide 반지름+14, 가운데로 약하게.
 * 처음 위치는 seedPosition(id) 이라 같은 데이터면 같은 모양. 300틱을 미리 돌려 열자마자 자리 잡힌 그림을 보여 준다
 */
export function createLayout(graph: SigunguGraphData, w: number, h: number): Layout {
  const nodes: LayoutNode[] = graph.nodes.map((n) => ({
    id: n.id,
    r: nodeRadius(n),
    ...seedPosition(n.id, w, h),
  }))
  // d3 가 링크 객체의 source/target 을 노드로 바꾸므로 복사본을 넘긴다
  const links: SimulationLinkDatum<LayoutNode>[] = graph.links.map((l) => ({
    source: l.source,
    target: l.target,
  }))
  const sim = forceSimulation(nodes)
    .force('charge', forceManyBody<LayoutNode>().strength(-260))
    .force(
      'link',
      forceLink<LayoutNode, SimulationLinkDatum<LayoutNode>>(links)
        .id((d) => d.id)
        .distance(80),
    )
    .force(
      'collide',
      forceCollide<LayoutNode>().radius((d) => d.r + 14),
    )
    .force('x', forceX<LayoutNode>(w / 2).strength(0.06))
    .force('y', forceY<LayoutNode>(h / 2).strength(0.06))
    .stop()
  for (let i = 0; i < TICKS; i++) {
    sim.tick()
    clamp(nodes, w, h)
  }
  return { nodes, byId: new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n])), sim }
}

/** 배치 + 끌기. 끄는 동안만 시뮬레이션을 다시 돌려 이웃이 따라오게 한다 */
export function useForceLayout(graph: SigunguGraphData, w: number, h: number) {
  const layout = useMemo(() => createLayout(graph, w, h), [graph, w, h])
  const [, redraw] = useReducer((n: number) => n + 1, 0)

  useEffect(() => {
    layout.sim.on('tick', () => {
      clamp(layout.nodes, w, h)
      redraw()
    })
    return () => {
      layout.sim.on('tick', null).stop()
    }
  }, [layout, w, h])

  const drag = useMemo(
    () => ({
      start(id: string) {
        const n = layout.byId.get(id)
        if (!n) return
        n.fx = n.x
        n.fy = n.y
        layout.sim.alphaTarget(0.3).restart()
      },
      move(id: string, x: number, y: number) {
        const n = layout.byId.get(id)
        if (!n) return
        n.fx = x
        n.fy = y
      },
      end(id: string) {
        const n = layout.byId.get(id)
        if (!n) return
        n.fx = null
        n.fy = null
        layout.sim.alphaTarget(0)
      },
    }),
    [layout],
  )
  return { layout, drag }
}
```

```ts
import type { RelationKey } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'

/** 사실 노드 색 (스펙 §3-2 표, Figma 변수와 같은 이름). 조용함·저녁 활기는 같은 회색 */

}

/** 칩·범례의 색 점 (동적 문자열 조합 금지 → 사전) */
export const RELATION_BG: Record<RelationKey, string> = {
  type: 'bg-explore-fact-type',
  coast: 'bg-explore-fact-coast',
```

### Task 20: RelationChips + SigunguGraph — ① 지식그래프 SVG

**Files:**

- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/RelationChips.tsx`
- Create: `src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph.tsx`
- Test: `src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph.test.tsx`

**Figma:** `434:9728` (RelationChips), `434:9744` (Graph 힘 기반 배치), 전체 `432:9600` 안 `432:9976`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: `RELATIONS`, `SigunguGraphData`, `GraphNode`, `RelationKey` (A), `useForceLayout`, `FACT_R`, `RELATION_FILL`, `RELATION_BG` (19), `GRAPH_W`, `GRAPH_H` (13)
- Produces:
  - `RelationChips({ on: ReadonlySet<RelationKey>; onToggle: (key: RelationKey) => void })` — `role="group"` 이름 "관계", 칩 `button[aria-pressed]`
  - `export type GraphPick = { kind: 'dong' | 'fact'; id: string }`
  - `SigunguGraph({ name: string; graph: SigunguGraphData; pick: GraphPick | null; onPick: (p: GraphPick | null) => void; hoveredDong: string | null; onHoverDong: (id: string | null) => void })` — `<svg role="group" aria-label="{name} 지식그래프">`, 노드 `g[role=button][data-kind="dong"|"fact"]`, 흐린 노드 `data-dim="true"`

- [ ] **Step 1: Figma 확인**

`get_design_context` 로 `434:9728` 을 보고, `get_screenshot` 으로 `432:9976` 을 본다(그래프는 벡터가 많아 스크린샷이 기준).

- 칩: gap 6, 줄바꿈. 알약 px 10 · py 5, 테두리 1 `border/default`, 색 점 8. 켬 = `bg/surface` + 12px Medium `text/primary` + 관계 색 점. 끔 = 바탕 없음 + 12px Regular `text/tertiary` + 회색 점
- 칩 글자는 **스펙 §3-2 표 이름**(파트 A `RELATIONS[].label`: 1위 유형·가까움·생활·조용함·저녁 활기)을 쓴다. Figma 칩 글자(유형·바닷가·생활권/관광지·조용함 등급·저녁 활기 등급)와 다르다
- 그래프 400×400. 동 노드 = 흰 원 + `brand/primary` 테두리 2.5, 이름은 원 아래 9px `text/tertiary`. 선택 동 = 테두리 3.5 + 이름 Bold `text/primary`. 사실 노드 = 지름 52 색 원 + 흰 글자 Bold 10px. 선 = 1px 회색, 강조 선 = 1.5px `text/primary`. 강조 밖 노드·선 18%

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph.test.tsx`

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import RelationChips from '@/features/explore/components/panel/RelationChips'
import SigunguGraph from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph'
import type { GraphPick } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph'
import { RELATIONS, buildSigunguGraph } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { GANGNEUNG_PINS, JUMUNJIN_ID, YEONGOK_ID } from '@/test/fixtures/explore'

const on = new Set(RELATIONS.filter((r) => r.defaultOn).map((r) => r.key))
const graph = buildSigunguGraph(GANGNEUNG_PINS, on)

const setup = (over: { pick?: GraphPick | null; hoveredDong?: string | null } = {}) => {
  const props = {
    name: '강릉시',
    graph,
    pick: over.pick ?? null,
    onPick: vi.fn(),
    hoveredDong: over.hoveredDong ?? null,
    onHoverDong: vi.fn(),
  }
  render(<SigunguGraph {...props} />)
  const svg = screen.getByRole('group', { name: '강릉시 지식그래프' })
  return { props, svg }
}

describe('RelationChips', () => {
  it('관계 5개, 기본 켬 3개(1위 유형·가까움·생활), 누르면 onToggle', async () => {
    const onToggle = vi.fn()
    render(<RelationChips on={on} onToggle={onToggle} />)
    const chips = within(screen.getByRole('group', { name: '관계' })).getAllByRole('button')
    expect(chips).toHaveLength(5)
    expect(chips.map((c) => c.getAttribute('aria-pressed'))).toEqual(
      RELATIONS.map((r) => String(r.defaultOn)),
    )
    await userEvent.click(chips[3])
    expect(onToggle).toHaveBeenCalledWith(RELATIONS[3].key)
  })
})

describe('SigunguGraph', () => {
  it('동 노드 21개 + 켜진 관계의 사실 노드, 시군구 노드 없음', () => {
    const { svg } = setup()
    expect(svg.querySelectorAll('[data-kind="dong"]')).toHaveLength(21)
    expect(svg.querySelectorAll('[data-kind="fact"]')).toHaveLength(graph.nodes.length - 21)
    expect(within(svg).queryByRole('button', { name: '강릉시' })).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('동 노드 클릭 → onPick(dong), 사실 노드 클릭 → onPick(fact), 빈 곳 클릭 → onPick(null)', async () => {
    const { svg, props } = setup()
    await userEvent.click(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '연곡면' }))
    expect(props.onPick).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ kind: 'dong', id: YEONGOK_ID })
    await userEvent.click(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '사실 바닷가' }))
    expect(props.onPick).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ kind: 'fact', id: 'coast' })
    await userEvent.click(svg)
    expect(props.onPick).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(null)
  })

  it('동 선택 → 그 동과 이어진 사실만 진하게, 나머지 흐림', () => {
    const { svg } = setup({ pick: { kind: 'dong', id: YEONGOK_ID } })
    const node = (name: string) => within(svg).getByRole('button', { name })
    expect(node('연곡면')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dim')
    expect(node('사실 바닷가')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dim')
    expect(node('사실 관광지')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dim')
    expect(node('강남동')).toHaveAttribute('data-dim', 'true')
    expect(node('사실 생활권')).toHaveAttribute('data-dim', 'true')
  })

  it('사실 선택 → 그 사실을 가진 동만 진하게', () => {
    const { svg } = setup({ pick: { kind: 'fact', id: 'coast' } })
    const node = (name: string) => within(svg).getByRole('button', { name })
    expect(node('주문진읍')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dim')
    expect(node('강남동')).toHaveAttribute('data-dim', 'true')
  })

  it('지도 핀 hover(hoveredDong) → 그래프에서 미리 강조, 노드 hover → onHoverDong', async () => {
    const { svg, props } = setup({ hoveredDong: JUMUNJIN_ID })
    expect(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '주문진읍' })).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dim')
    expect(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '연곡면' })).toHaveAttribute('data-dim', 'true')
    await userEvent.hover(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '연곡면' }))
    expect(props.onHoverDong).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(YEONGOK_ID)
    await userEvent.unhover(within(svg).getByRole('button', { name: '연곡면' }))
    expect(props.onHoverDong).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(null)
  })
})
```

(`사실 바닷가` 노드 id 가 `coast`, `사실 생활권` 라벨이 `생활권` 인 것은 파트 A 인터페이스의 사실 노드 id·라벨 규칙을 따른 것.)

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { RELATIONS } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { RelationKey } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { RELATION_BG } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/relationColor'

type Props = { on: ReadonlySet<RelationKey>; onToggle: (key: RelationKey) => void }

/** ① 관계 칩 5개 켜기·끄기 (Figma 434:9728). 이름은 스펙 §3-2 표 (RELATIONS) */
export default function RelationChips({ on, onToggle }: Props) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label={EXPLORE_COPY.relationChipsLabel}
      className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap gap-1.5"
    >
      {RELATIONS.map((r) => {
        const active = on.has(r.key)
        return (
          <button
            key={r.key}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={active}
            onClick={() => onToggle(r.key)}
            className={`border-border-default text-caption-xs inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1.25 ${active ? 'bg-bg-surface text-text-primary font-medium' : 'text-text-tertiary'}`}
          >
            <span
              aria-hidden
              className={`size-2 rounded-full ${active ? RELATION_BG[r.key] : 'bg-border-strong'}`}
            />
            {r.label}
          </button>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph.tsx`

```tsx
import { useRef } from 'react'
import type { KeyboardEvent, MouseEvent, PointerEvent } from 'react'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { FACT_R, useForceLayout } from '@/features/explore/forceLayout'
import type { GraphNode, RelationKey, SigunguGraphData } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { GRAPH_H, GRAPH_W } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'
import { RELATION_FILL } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/relationColor'

export type GraphPick = { kind: 'dong' | 'fact'; id: string }

type Props = {
  name: string
  graph: SigunguGraphData
  pick: GraphPick | null
  onPick: (pick: GraphPick | null) => void
  hoveredDong: string | null
  onHoverDong: (dongId: string | null) => void
}

/** 강조 대상 노드 id (그 노드 + 선으로 이어진 이웃). focus 가 없으면 null = 모두 진하게 */
function litIds(graph: SigunguGraphData, focusId: string | null): Set<string> | null {
  if (!focusId) return null
  const ids = new Set([focusId])
  for (const l of graph.links) {
    if (l.source === focusId) ids.add(l.target)
    if (l.target === focusId) ids.add(l.source)
  }
  return ids
}

const DIM = 0.18
const DRAG_PX = 3

/**
 * ① 시군구 지식그래프 (Figma 434:9744, 스펙 §3-2). 동 + 켜진 관계의 사실 노드, d3-force 배치, 끌어서 옮기기.
 * 클릭은 그래프 안 강조만 한다(서버 요청 없음). 지도 핀 hover 는 hoveredDong 으로 받아 미리 강조
 */
export default function SigunguGraph({
  name,
  graph,
  pick,
  onPick,
  hoveredDong,
  onHoverDong,
}: Props) {
  const { layout, drag } = useForceLayout(graph, GRAPH_W, GRAPH_H)
  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null)
  const dragging = useRef<{ id: string; x0: number; y0: number; moved: boolean } | null>(null)

  const focusId = hoveredDong ?? pick?.id ?? null
  const lit = litIds(graph, focusId)

  const toSvg = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    const rect = svgRef.current!.getBoundingClientRect()
    return {
      x: ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * GRAPH_W,
      y: ((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * GRAPH_H,
    }
  }
  const onPointerDown = (id: string) => (e: PointerEvent<SVGGElement>) => {
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId)
    dragging.current = { id, x0: e.clientX, y0: e.clientY, moved: false }
  }
  const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    const d = dragging.current
    if (!d) return
    if (!d.moved) {
      if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - d.x0, e.clientY - d.y0) < DRAG_PX) return
      d.moved = true
      drag.start(d.id)
    }
    const p = toSvg(e)
    drag.move(d.id, p.x, p.y)
  }
  const onPointerUp = () => {
    const d = dragging.current
    if (d?.moved) drag.end(d.id)
  }
  const pickOf = (n: GraphNode): GraphPick => ({
    kind: n.kind === 'dong' ? 'dong' : 'fact',
    id: n.id,
  })
  const onNodeClick = (n: GraphNode) => (e: MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    const d = dragging.current
    dragging.current = null
    if (d?.moved) return
    onPick(pickOf(n))
  }
  const onNodeKey = (n: GraphNode) => (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return
    e.preventDefault()
    onPick(pickOf(n))
  }

  return (
    <svg
      ref={svgRef}
      role="group"
      aria-label={EXPLORE_COPY.graphLabel(name)}
      viewBox={`0 0 ${GRAPH_W} ${GRAPH_H}`}
      className="h-100 w-full shrink-0 touch-none select-none"
      onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
      onClick={() => onPick(null)}
    >
      <g>
        {graph.links.map((l) => {
          const a = layout.byId.get(l.source)
          const b = layout.byId.get(l.target)
          if (!a || !b) return null
          const on = focusId === null || l.source === focusId || l.target === focusId
          const strong = focusId !== null && on
          return (
            <line
              key={`${l.source}|${l.target}`}
              x1={a.x}
              y1={a.y}
              x2={b.x}
              y2={b.y}
              className={strong ? 'stroke-text-primary' : 'stroke-border-strong'}
              strokeWidth={strong ? 1.5 : 1}
              opacity={on ? 1 : DIM}
            />
          )
        })}
      </g>
      {graph.nodes.map((n) => {
        const p = layout.byId.get(n.id)
        if (!p) return null
        const isDong = n.kind === 'dong'
        const on = lit === null || lit.has(n.id)
        const selected = focusId === n.id
        return (
          <g
            key={n.id}
            role="button"
            tabIndex={0}
            aria-label={isDong ? n.label : EXPLORE_COPY.factNode(n.label)}
            aria-pressed={pick?.id === n.id}
            data-kind={isDong ? 'dong' : 'fact'}
            data-dim={on ? undefined : 'true'}
            opacity={on ? 1 : DIM}
            transform={`translate(${p.x} ${p.y})`}
            className="cursor-pointer outline-none"
            onPointerDown={onPointerDown(n.id)}
            onPointerUp={onPointerUp}
            onClick={onNodeClick(n)}
            onKeyDown={onNodeKey(n)}
            onPointerEnter={isDong ? () => onHoverDong(n.id) : undefined}
            onPointerLeave={isDong ? () => onHoverDong(null) : undefined}
          >
            {isDong ? (
              <>
                <circle
                  r={p.r}
                  className="fill-bg-surface stroke-brand-primary"
                  strokeWidth={selected ? 3.5 : 2.5}
                />
                <text
                  y={p.r + 10}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  className={`text-explore-3xs ${selected ? 'fill-text-primary font-bold' : 'fill-text-tertiary'}`}
                >
                  {n.label}
                </text>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <circle r={FACT_R} fill={RELATION_FILL[n.kind as RelationKey]} />
                <text
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  dominantBaseline="central"
                  className="fill-text-inverse text-explore-2xs font-bold"
                >
                  {n.label}
                </text>
              </>
            )}
          </g>
        )
      })}
    </svg>
  )
}
```

```tsx
    ).toEqual(['관광지', '바닷가', '장기체류형'])
    expect(card).toHaveTextContent('공통 3')
    expect(card).toHaveTextContent('강동면 · 사천면 · 옥계면')
    await userEvent.click(within(card).getByRole('button', { name: '동네 자세히 보기' }))

    expect(card).toHaveTextContent('주문진읍')

  it('그래프에서 빠진 노드(관계 끔)는 카드 없음', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <SelectionCard
        graph={graph}
        pins={GANGNEUNG_PINS}
        pick={{ kind: 'fact', id: 'quiet:B' }}
        onOpenDong={() => {}}
      />,
    )
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement()
  })
})

describe('SigunguGraphSkeleton', () => {

    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: '관계' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: '강릉시 지식그래프' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText('동 (원 크기 = 1위 유형 점수)')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(/생활권 = 생활권 지수 0.7 이상/)).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: '동네 자세히 보기' })).not.toBeInTheDocument()

  it('pick 이 있으면 카드', () => {
    render(<SigunguPanel {...base} pick={{ kind: 'dong', id: YEONGOK_ID }} />)
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '동네 자세히 보기' })).toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SelectionCard.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { factDongs, sharedFacts } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { GraphNode, RelationKey, SigunguGraphData } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { PinDong } from '@/features/explore/types'
import type { GraphPick } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph'
import { RELATION_BG } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/relationColor'

type Props = {
  graph: SigunguGraphData
  pins: PinDong[]
  pick: GraphPick
  onOpenDong: (dongId: string) => void
}

const COMMON_MAX = 3
const CARD =
  'border-border-default bg-bg-surface flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 rounded-xl border p-3.5'

/** 이 노드와 선으로 이어진 노드 (링크 방향과 관계없이) */
function neighbors(graph: SigunguGraphData, id: string): GraphNode[] {
  const byId = new Map(graph.nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]))
  return graph.links.flatMap((l) => {
    const other = l.source === id ? l.target : l.target === id ? l.source : null
    const node = other ? byId.get(other) : undefined
    return node ? [node] : []
  })
}

/** ① 선택 카드 (Figma 434:9855). 동: 이어진 사실 + 공통 사실 많은 동 최대 3 + [동네 자세히 보기]. 사실: 이어진 동 목록 (스펙 §3-2) */
export default function SelectionCard({ graph, pins, pick, onOpenDong }: Props) {
  const node = graph.nodes.find((n) => n.id === pick.id)
  if (!node) return null

  if (pick.kind === 'fact') {
    const ids = new Set(factDongs(graph, pick.id))
    const names = pins.filter((p) => ids.has(p.id)).map((p) => p.name)
    return (
      <section aria-label={node.label} className={CARD}>
        <h3 className="text-title-md text-text-primary font-bold">
          {EXPLORE_COPY.cardFactTitle(node.label, names.length)}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-caption-xs text-text-secondary">{names.join(' · ')}</p>
      </section>
    )
  }

  const pin = pins.find((p) => p.id === pick.id)
  const facts = neighbors(graph, pick.id)
  const common = sharedFacts(graph, pick.id).slice(0, COMMON_MAX)
  return (
    <section aria-label={node.label} className={CARD}>
      <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-title-md text-text-primary font-bold">{node.label}</h3>
        {pin?.topType && pin.topScore !== null && (
          <span className="text-caption-xs text-text-secondary">{`${pin.topType} ${pin.topScore}`}</span>
        )}
      </div>
      <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        {facts.map((f) => (
          <li
            key={f.id}
            className="bg-bg-subtle text-explore-xs text-text-primary inline-flex items-center gap-1.25 rounded-full px-2 py-0.75 font-medium"
          >
            <span
              aria-hidden
              className={`size-2 rounded-full ${RELATION_BG[f.kind as RelationKey]}`}
            />
            {f.label}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {common.length > 0 && (
        <p className="text-caption-xs flex gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-text-primary font-bold">
            {EXPLORE_COPY.cardCommon(common[0].common.length)}
          </span>
          <span className="text-text-secondary">{common.map((c) => c.name).join(' · ')}</span>
        </p>
      )}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => onOpenDong(pick.id)}
        className="bg-brand-primary text-text-inverse text-explore-sm w-full rounded-lg py-2.5 font-bold"
      >
        {EXPLORE_COPY.openDong}
      </button>
    </section>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/GraphLegend.tsx`

```tsx
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import type { RelationKey } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { RELATION_BG } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/relationColor'

type Props = { on: ReadonlySet<RelationKey> }

/** ① 그래프 아래 범례 (Figma 434:9874). 켜진 관계의 사실 색만 */
export default function GraphLegend({ on }: Props) {
  const items: { key: string; label: string; dot: string }[] = [
    {
      key: 'dong',
      label: EXPLORE_COPY.legendDong,
      dot: 'bg-bg-surface border-brand-primary border-2',
    },
  ]
  if (on.has('type'))
    items.push({ key: 'type', label: EXPLORE_COPY.legendType, dot: RELATION_BG.type })
  if (on.has('coast'))
    items.push({ key: 'coast', label: EXPLORE_COPY.legendCoast, dot: RELATION_BG.coast })
  if (on.has('life'))
    items.push({ key: 'life', label: EXPLORE_COPY.legendLife, dot: RELATION_BG.life })
  if (on.has('quiet') || on.has('evening'))
    items.push({ key: 'grade', label: EXPLORE_COPY.legendGrade, dot: RELATION_BG.quiet })
  return (
    <ul className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      {items.map((i) => (
        <li
          key={i.key}
          className="text-explore-xs text-text-secondary inline-flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          <span aria-hidden className={`size-2.25 rounded-full ${i.dot}`} />
          {i.label}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraphSkeleton.tsx`

```tsx
import Block from '@/features/explore/components/Skeleton'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { seedPosition } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import { GRAPH_H, GRAPH_W, SIGUNGU_HEADER_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = { ids: string[] }

const CHIP_W = ['w-15.5', 'w-18', 'w-27', 'w-23', 'w-27']

/**
 * ① 로딩 (Figma L2 429:9936). 원은 동 경계 파일의 동 수만큼, 위치는 실제 배치의 씨앗(seedPosition)과 같아
 * 핀 응답이 오면 그 자리에서 퍼진다. 선은 없다 (스펙 §9-1)
 */
export default function SigunguGraphSkeleton({ ids }: Props) {
  return (
    <div
      data-testid="graph-skeleton"
      aria-busy="true"
      className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3.5 pt-5"
    >
      <span className="sr-only">{EXPLORE_COPY.loadingSr}</span>
      <div className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1.5 ${SIGUNGU_HEADER_H}`}>
        <Block className="h-6 w-22.5" />
        <Block className="h-3 w-45" />
      </div>
      <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        {CHIP_W.map((w, i) => (
          <Block key={i} shape="pill" className={`h-6.5 ${w}`} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <svg aria-hidden viewBox={`0 0 ${GRAPH_W} ${GRAPH_H}`} className="h-100 w-full shrink-0">
        {ids.map((id) => {
          const p = seedPosition(id, GRAPH_W, GRAPH_H)
          return (
            <circle
              key={id}
              data-skeleton-node
              cx={p.x}
              cy={p.y}
              r={8}
              className="fill-explore-skeleton-base"
            />
          )
        })}
      </svg>
      <Block className="h-2.5 w-75" />
    </div>
  )
}
```

`src/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguPanel.tsx`

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { EXPLORE_COPY } from '@/features/explore/copy'
import { buildSigunguGraph } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { RelationKey } from '@/features/explore/sigunguGraph'
import type { PinDong } from '@/features/explore/types'
import GraphLegend from '@/features/explore/components/panel/GraphLegend'
import RelationChips from '@/features/explore/components/panel/RelationChips'
import SelectionCard from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SelectionCard'
import SigunguGraph from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph'
import type { GraphPick } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SigunguGraph'
import SourceLine from '@/features/explore/components/panel/SourceLine'
import { SIGUNGU_HEADER_H } from '@/features/explore/components/panel/panelLayout'

type Props = {
  name: string
  province: string
  pins: PinDong[]
  relsOn: ReadonlySet<RelationKey>
  onToggleRel: (key: RelationKey) => void
  pick: GraphPick | null
  onPick: (pick: GraphPick | null) => void
  hoveredDong: string | null
  onHoverDong: (dongId: string | null) => void
  onOpenDong: (dongId: string) => void
}

/** ① 패널 (Figma 432:9976). 시군구 노드는 그리지 않는다 (스펙 §3-2) */
export default function SigunguPanel({
  name,
  province,
  pins,
  relsOn,
  onToggleRel,
  pick,
  onPick,
  hoveredDong,
  onHoverDong,
  onOpenDong,
}: Props) {
  const graph = useMemo(() => buildSigunguGraph(pins, relsOn), [pins, relsOn])
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3.5 pt-5">
      <header className={`flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1 ${SIGUNGU_HEADER_H}`}>
        <h2 className="text-heading-xl text-text-primary leading-6">{name}</h2>
        <p className="text-caption-xs text-text-secondary">
          {EXPLORE_COPY.sigunguSub(province, pins.length)}
        </p>
      </header>
      <RelationChips on={relsOn} onToggle={onToggleRel} />
      <SigunguGraph
        name={name}
        graph={graph}
        pick={pick}
        onPick={onPick}
        hoveredDong={hoveredDong}
        onHoverDong={onHoverDong}
      />
      {pick && <SelectionCard graph={graph} pins={pins} pick={pick} onOpenDong={onOpenDong} />}
      <GraphLegend on={relsOn} />
      <SourceLine>{EXPLORE_COPY.sigunguSource}</SourceLine>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useSigunguMeta } from '@/features/explore/useSigunguMeta'

function wrapper({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })
  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
}

describe('useSigunguMeta', () => {
  it('시군구 목록(tier mock)에서 이름·시도를 찾는다', async () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useSigunguMeta('51150'), { wrapper })
    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(result.current).toEqual({ name: '강릉시', province: '강원특별자치도' }),
    )
  })
  it('sgg 가 없으면 null', () => {
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useSigunguMeta(null), { wrapper })
    expect(result.current).toBeNull()
  })
})
```
